Catalogue of materials

Everything on VistaEd: 2812 interactive lessons with exam-style questions, 38 virtual laboratories, simulators, games and examinations. Visitors can browse this list. To open and use the materials, create an account. It is free and opens straight away.

Virtual laboratories 38 labs
  • 🔒 Enzyme activity laboratory
    Molecular biology · MYP 4–5 · IB DP · IGCSE · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 9–10 — Change temperature, pH, substrate, enzyme concentration and inhibitors. Measure initial rates, find the optimum and compare inhibitors.
  • 🔒 Osmosis laboratory: plant and animal cells
    Biology · MYP 3–5 · IB DP · IGCSE · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 8–12 — Put potato cells and red blood cells in sucrose solutions. Track water potential, turgor, plasmolysis and haemolysis; find the isotonic point.
  • 🔒 DNA replication laboratory
    Molecular biology · MYP 5 · IB DP · IGCSE · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 11–12 — Use helicase, primase, DNA polymerases and ligase to copy DNA, build leading and lagging strands, then measure how proofreading cuts errors.
  • 🔒 Heart and circulation laboratory
    Human physiology · MYP 3–5 · IB DP · IGCSE · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 10 — Watch the cardiac cycle with live pressure traces and valves. Change heart rate, contractility and valve disease; measure stroke volume and cardiac output.
  • 🔒 Nerve impulse laboratory: neural signalling
    Human physiology · MYP 4–5 · IB DP · IGCSE · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 12 — Stimulate an axon and watch Na⁺ and K⁺ channels open. Find the threshold and refractory period, and measure how diameter, myelin, temperature and drugs change impulse speed.
  • 🔒 Chemical signalling laboratory: ligands and receptors
    Molecular biology · MYP 4–5 · IB DP · IGCSE · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 12 — Add adrenaline, insulin or oestradiol to target cells. Watch transmembrane and intracellular receptors, second messengers and amplification; plot dose–response curves and test a blocker.
  • 🔒 Miller–Urey laboratory: the origin of organic molecules
    Biology · MYP 4–5 · IB DP · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 12 — Run Miller and Urey's 1953 spark experiment: boil water, spark reducing, neutral or oxidising gases, watch the trap turn red-brown and identify amino acids by paper chromatography.
  • 🔒 Properties of water laboratory: hydrogen bonds in action
    Biology · MYP 3–5 · IB DP · IGCSE · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 8–10 — Animations of polarity and hydrogen bonds, cohesion and surface tension, adhesion and capillary action, heat capacity, water as a solvent and floating ice. Measure capillary rise and heating.
  • 🔒 Photosynthetic pigments laboratory: chromatography and Rf values
    Biology · IB DP · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 9–10 — Separate leaf pigments on a TLC plate or paper, watch the solvent front rise, measure each band and calculate Rf values to identify carotene, chlorophylls and xanthophylls.
  • 🔒 Photosynthesis laboratory: limiting factors and the action spectrum
    Biology · MYP 4–5 · IB DP · IGCSE · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 9–10 — Count oxygen bubbles from pondweed as you move the lamp, change CO₂ and temperature, and use coloured filters. Find the limiting factor and build an action spectrum.
  • 🔒 Cell respiration laboratory: the respirometer
    Biology · MYP 4–5 · IB DP · IGCSE · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 10 — Germinating seeds take in oxygen and the coloured liquid moves. Change temperature, seeds and mass; calculate oxygen uptake and the rate of respiration.
  • 🔒 Tropisms laboratory: phototropism, gravitropism and auxin
    Biology · MYP 3–5 · IB DP · IGCSE · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 11–12 — Light seedlings from one side, lay them down or spin them on a clinostat. Remove or cover the tip, add mica or auxin agar; watch auxin move and measure the angle of curvature.
  • 🔒 Microscope laboratory: mounts, measuring cells, mitotic index and stomata
    Biology · MYP 3–5 · IB DP · IGCSE · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 7–12 — Make temporary mounts, stain and focus; measure cells with an eyepiece graticule; count mitosis in root tips and tumours, stomata in leaf casts; view arteries, veins, stems and roots.
  • 🔒 Ecological sampling laboratory: quadrats, Lincoln index and chi-squared
    Environmental science · MYP 4–5 · IB DP · IGCSE · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 9–11 — Place random quadrats in a field of daisies, mark and recapture beetles, and test two plant species for association with a chi-squared test.
  • 🔒 Transect laboratory: distribution and range of tolerance
    Environmental science · MYP 4–5 · IB DP · IGCSE · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 11 — Walk a belt transect from woodland into a meadow, measure light and soil moisture, record plant cover and correlate distribution with an abiotic factor.
  • 🔒 Population growth laboratory: yeast and duckweed
    Environmental science · IB DP · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 11 — Grow yeast (haemocytometer counts) or duckweed; change food, temperature and starting number; plot exponential and sigmoid growth on normal and log scales.
  • 🔒 Mesocosm laboratory: what keeps an ecosystem stable?
    Environmental science · MYP 4–5 · IB DP · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 9–11 — Seal pondweed, snails and decomposers in a jar; change light and numbers; track oxygen, carbon dioxide and populations for 12 weeks.
  • 🔒 Natural and sexual selection laboratory: Endler's guppies
    Biology · IB DP · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 12 — Model Endler's guppy ponds: change the predator and the gravel, switch female choice on or off, and track the mean number of spots over generations.
  • 🔒 Lung volumes laboratory: spirometry
    Human physiology · MYP 4–5 · IB DP · IGCSE · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 10 — Breathe into a spirometer; read tidal volume, IRV, ERV and vital capacity from the trace; compare people and the effect of exercise.
  • 🔒 Pulse rate laboratory: exercise and recovery
    Human physiology · MYP 3–5 · IB DP · IGCSE · Kenya CBE Grade 10 — Feel the radial or carotid pulse or use a pulse oximeter; measure heart rate at rest and in recovery after exercise for fitter and less fit volunteers.
  • 🔒 Range of motion laboratory: measuring joint angles
    Human physiology · IB DP · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 12 — Use a goniometer to measure the range of motion of the elbow, knee, shoulder and hip in different planes, and compare people.
  • 🔒 PCR and gel electrophoresis laboratory
    Molecular biology · IB DP · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 12 — Run a thermocycler, then a gel with a DNA ladder; solve a forensic case, a paternity test and a virus test.
  • 🔒 Genetic crosses laboratory: dihybrid ratios and chi-squared
    Biology · IB DP · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 12 — Count purple, yellow, smooth and wrinkled kernels on maize cobs and test the 9:3:3:1 and 1:1:1:1 ratios with chi-squared; discover linked genes.
  • 🔒 Acid–base titration laboratory
    Chemistry · MYP 4–5 · IB DP · IGCSE · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 7–11 — Titrate strong and weak acids and bases with indicators and a pH meter. Plot the pH curve, find the equivalence point, pKa and an unknown concentration.
  • 🔒 Rates of reaction laboratory: marble chips and acid
    Chemistry · MYP 4–5 · IB DP · IGCSE · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 11 — Collect carbon dioxide in a gas syringe while you change acid concentration, chip size and temperature. Find initial rates, the order of reaction and explain with collision theory.
  • 🔒 Electrolysis laboratory: products and Faraday's law
    Chemistry · MYP 4–5 · IB DP · IGCSE · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 12 — Watch ions move to the electrodes in copper sulfate, brine, dilute acid and silver nitrate. Weigh the electrodes, collect gases, write half-equations and find the Faraday constant.
  • 🔒 Chemical equilibrium laboratory: Le Châtelier's principle
    Chemistry · MYP 5 · IB DP · IGCSE · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 12 — Heat, cool and compress a sealed syringe of brown NO₂ and colourless N₂O₄. Watch the colour shift, calculate Kc and decide whether the reaction is endothermic.
  • 🔒 Enthalpy of combustion laboratory: burning alcohols
    Chemistry · MYP 4–5 · IB DP · IGCSE · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 12 — Burn methanol to pentan-1-ol under a copper can of water. Use Q = mcΔT to find enthalpies of combustion, the trend per CH₂ group and how much heat is lost.
  • 🔒 Transpiration laboratory: the bubble potometer
    Biology · MYP 3–5 · IGCSE · Edexcel · IB DP · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 10 — Time a bubble in a potometer as you change light, temperature, humidity and wind, grease leaf surfaces, and convert distance to volume of water taken up.
  • 🔒 Food tests laboratory: sugars, starch, protein and fat
    Biology · MYP 1–5 · IGCSE · Edexcel · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 9–10 — Run Benedict's, iodine, biuret and emulsion tests on real foods, build a glucose colour scale and identify the nutrients in an unknown food.
  • 🔒 Diffusion laboratory: agar cubes and surface area to volume ratio
    Biology · MYP 3–5 · IGCSE · Edexcel · IB DP · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 8–10 — Drop pink agar cubes of different sizes into acid, cut them open and measure how far the acid has diffused; link SA:V ratio to exchange surfaces.
  • 🔒 Identifying ions laboratory: flame tests, precipitates and gas tests
    Chemistry · MYP 3–5 · IGCSE · Edexcel · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 10–11 — Do flame tests, sodium hydroxide, silver nitrate, barium chloride, carbonate and ammonium tests, then identify four unknown salts.
  • 🔒 Paper chromatography laboratory: food colourings and Rf values
    Chemistry · MYP 1–5 · IGCSE · Edexcel · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 7 — Watch the solvent climb the paper, measure spots and calculate Rf values in three solvents to identify the dyes in sweets and a drink.
  • 🔒 Reactivity laboratory: displacement reactions and electrochemical cells
    Chemistry · MYP 3–5 · IGCSE · Edexcel · IB DP · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 10–12 — Add metals to salt solutions and measure the temperature rise, then build cells from pairs of metals and measure the voltage; rank six metals.
  • 🔒 Electric circuits laboratory: I–V characteristics, series and parallel
    Physics · MYP 3–5 · IGCSE · Edexcel · IB DP · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 7–11 — Measure current and p.d. for a resistor, filament lamp, diode and thermistor, plot I–V graphs, and test series and parallel rules.
  • 🔒 Hooke's law laboratory: springs, rubber bands and the elastic limit
    Physics · MYP 3–5 · IGCSE · Edexcel · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 10 — Load springs and a rubber band, measure extension, find the spring constant and the elastic limit, and compare springs in series and parallel.
  • 🔒 Refraction laboratory: Snell's law and total internal reflection
    Physics · MYP 3–5 · IGCSE · Edexcel · IB DP · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 9–12 — Shine a ray into glass, perspex, water or diamond, measure angles, find the refractive index and the critical angle, and see total internal reflection.
  • 🔒 Force and acceleration laboratory: Newton's second law
    Physics · MYP 3–5 · IGCSE · Edexcel · IB DP · A Level · Kenya CBE Grade 10–12 — Pull a trolley through two light gates with hanging masses, calculate acceleration, and test F = ma with and without friction.
